local obf_XOR = (obf_bitlib and obf_bitlib.bxor) or function(a, b)
	a, b = a % 4294967296, b % 4294967296;
	local result, power = 0, 1;
	while (a > 0) or (b > 0) do
		local left, right = a % 2, b % 2;
		if (left ~= right) then
			result = result + power;
		end
		a, b, power = (a - left) / 2, (b - right) / 2, power * 2;
	end
	return result;
end;
